The Extensions list rows moved onto `createRow()`, which writes the field, so the rows' identity went from `data:data-sb-subpage-row=<container>` to `data:data-sb-editor-field=subpage@as:<title>`, a key that changes when a title does. The stack now passes the container id as the field (`setRows` in `public/scripts/sillybunny-settings-subpage.js`), and the fixture records `data:data-sb-editor-field=<container>`. A field name that several rows share is suffixed with the folded name.
  - **A dependent set in a closed expander was recorded disabled:** `enableDependentSets()` skips a set that is not shown and a switch that is not visible, so Dynamic Temperature's range rows (a `data-sb-enabled-by` set inside a closed sampler expander) were recorded `disabled`, without `keys` or `selfSaves`, and no round trip ran on them. `collectSection()` now enables the sets again once `expandAll()` has opened the expanders, and its caller gives those switches back (`tests/helpers/settings-inventory-steps.js`, `tests/helpers/settings-inventory-run.js`). A control that must stay usable inside a dependent set that is off (the sampler help toggle, which the harness presses to open) carries `data-sb-keeps-sensitive`, and `setRowInsensitive()` skips it.
- **An open step returns when the page is built (F-304):** Appearance, Interface, Messages (all built by `sillybunny-appearance.js`), Formatting and Presets (`sillybunny-backend-pages.js`), Sampling, Data & Security and Logs build after their module loads, and a read before then sees the raw upstream rows or a loading status. `openSettingsTab()` (`tests/helpers/surfaces/steps.js`), which every settings surface's `open` goes through, calls `waitForPageBuilt()`: no `aria-busy` on `[data-sb-panel="<tab>"]` or on a direct child of its scroller. Appearance, Interface and Messages (`initializeSettingsPanel`) and Formatting and Presets (`ensureBackendPages`) had no mark; `markPanelsBusyUntilBuilt()` (`public/scripts/sillybunny-tabs.js`) sets it on their panels until the build settles. CPU load alone (128 busy loops, load about 150, the whole inventory at `-s 3 -w 8`) did not reproduce the failure on the old code; slowing the module imports does. Test: `tests/audit/settings-open-wait.e2e.js` delays the five modules by 6 s and reads each page the moment its open step returns (7 of 8 fail on the old code; Sampling already waited through the logit bias step).
- **The design documents state patterns, and a test holds them to it:** `DESIGN.md` and `DESIGN.json` describe patterns, roles and conditions, never one feature. `tests/unit/design-documents-generality.test.js` fails when either names a feature, page, window or bundled extension from its list; add a name to the list when a feature gets one. What is true of one feature lives in `docs/feature-design-rules.md` (`6eb9df89e`, `87f1dde65`).
  - A deviation has one form: what the reference does; what SillyBunny does, because of a reason; when to review it. A deviation without a reason is not written by an agent: the user gives the reason, or the app follows the reference.
  - The documents may state a rule that the code does not meet yet, with a clause "Existing … move to this rule as each surface is rebuilt". Delete the clause in the commit that makes the rule true.
- **The parity rules follow the design documents:** a canonical value in `tests/helpers/parity/` that cites `DESIGN.md` changes when the documents do. Record the allow-list again afterwards (`SB_PARITY_RECORD=1 SB_CENSUS_DIR=<dir> SURFACE_CHECKS=parity` with `audit/surface-audit.e2e.js` under the audit config, then `node scripts/update-parity-allowlist.js --census <dir> --write --allow-increase`), and state the rise in the commit (`f56c6d920`: 1,441 to 1,578 temporary entries for the decided values).
- **The reference of the design documents:** both record a libadwaita release and a commit of the GNOME HIG, and `tests/unit/design-documents-reference.test.js` fails when they disagree or lose either. `node scripts/check-design-reference.js` asks gitlab.gnome.org whether the reference has moved; it needs the network and is never run by the test suite (`87f1dde65`). The Design Reference workflow (`.github/workflows/design-reference.yml`) runs it when `staging` moves to a new `X.Y.0-dev` version, and keeps one open `design-reference` issue while the reference has moved.
- **A settings row gets its metrics from tokens, and a literal in a more specific rule hides them:** `.ds-row` (`public/css/sillybunny-tabs.css`) reads `--sb-density-row-min-height`, `--sb-density-row-padding-*`, `--sb-density-row-gap` and `--sb-density-row-text-gap`, and the page reads `--sb-settings-group-gap`, `--sb-settings-heading-gap` and `--sb-settings-inset*` (`public/css/sillybunny-theme.css`). Removing the old `#sb-settings-page .ds-row-switch` rule showed the older rules under it: `.checkbox_label` and `.range-block` rules scoped by `#openai_api`, the `[data-tg-samplers]` card column, `.sb-topbar-label-option { min-height: auto }` at 768px and below, and the `(pointer: coarse)` list that gives `.ds-row` in Sampling a literal 44px. Each now excludes `.ds-row` or reads the token. The Sampling rebuild later deleted the `[data-tg-samplers]` card column and the Sampling 44px list, so its rows read the tokens alone. A new row rule is checked by `expectSwitchGeometry` (`tests/sillybunny-backend-settings.e2e.js`, `tests/sillybunny-settings-consistency.e2e.js`), `tests/sillybunny-connections-layout.e2e.js` (rows compared with the shared row) and the `row` part of `tests/helpers/parity/settings.js`.
  - **A row has no second line of controls:** the wire control of a sampler (Auto, Send, Omit) was once a full-width line inside its slider row, which made the row taller and put the slider 22px above its midpoint; the row-geometry check of the parity spec failed for four Chat Completion samplers. It is a toggle row of its own after the slider row (`addWireRow()` in `public/scripts/sillybunny-sampling.js`), so the check needs no exception. Run the parity spec of every surface a change touches, not only the one the task names.
- **A dialog row takes the same metrics as a settings row, and a fallback rule at higher specificity hides them:** the dialog family's `row` part (`tests/helpers/parity/dialog.js`, `ROW_METRICS`, shared with the settings family) holds the 50px minimum and the 6px gap of every `.ds-row` in a dialog, which found rows at 44px with a 16px inset (`.sb-dialog .ds-row.checkbox_label`), a 12px gap (`dialog.popup.sb-dialog .checkbox_label`, the fallback for unconverted labels, now `:not(.ds-row)`), an 8px gap on stacked rows, a 44px Run Test button row (`regex/debugger.css`) and 10px block padding on property rows (`.ds-row-property`, which the settings groups had overridden). The dialog family holds at most 20 properties (`tests/unit/parity-allowlist.test.js`), so the part measures the height and the gap only; the padding is the settings family's. The art dialogs keep 44px, 16px and 12px through `.sb-dialog:has(.sbArtFigure)`, because their height decides where the art stands on the pixel grid (`tests/art-pixel-evenness.e2e.js`). Tests: `tests/audit/surface-audit.e2e.js` (parity, `SURFACE_FAMILY=dialog`).
- **A surface with no status page is not measured as one:** `status.home.about-unknown` is a property row of the About window and `status.sampling.unavailable` is an inert row of a boxed list with a badge, so the status census read the dialog frame's title wrapper, its Close button, the About rows and the badge icon as status parts. Both are in the family's `skip` (`tests/helpers/parity/status.js`) with the reason, and their 16 entries are gone. A surface whose open step shows no `.sb-status-page`, heading or action is a candidate for the same.
  - **No width breakpoint:** `.ds-row` wraps (`flex-wrap`), the text takes `flex: 1 1 var(--sb-row-title-min)` and a control keeps its own basis, so a control drops under its title only when both do not fit, and the threshold follows the text size. A row whose children cannot wrap sets `flex-wrap: nowrap`: the floating entry row, `.ds-row--stacked`, and the extension manager row (`.extension_block`, whose text block is `display: contents` over `nowrap` children; with wrapping on, the switch dropped to a second line).
  - **A row title breaks only at a word boundary (F-306):** `.ds-row-text` had `min-width: 0` and `overflow-wrap: anywhere`. With `anywhere`, the title's minimum width is one letter, so where the title shares its text box with a button (the sampler help toggle, 44px on a phone) the title shrank by a few pixels and "Temperature" broke as "Temperatu/re" at 390 and 360px. The title column now keeps its automatic minimum (its longest word and the button), `max-width: 100%` clamps that to the row, and `overflow-wrap: break-word` breaks a word only when it cannot fit on a line of its own; the row wraps its control under the title first. Not changed: the entry rows (`.ds-row-entry__caption`) and the legacy flex-container labels (`min-width: min(var(--sb-row-title-min), 100%)`), which break a word wider than their own 7em box. Test: `tests/sillybunny-row-title-wrap.e2e.js` (a touch phone at 390, 360 and 320px, Chat Completion and Aphrodite, every explanation open; 4 of 9 fail on the old sheet).
  - **A dialog with art keeps its rows:** the Welcome page stands on the device pixel grid only at the heights its rows gave it, and rows of 50px with a 57.3px subtitle row made `tests/art-pixel-evenness.e2e.js` fail at scale 3. `.sb-dialog:has(.sbArtFigure)` in `sillybunny-theme.css` resets the row tokens to 44px, 16px and 12px and restores the 52px subtitle row.
  - **Editor sheets that pad their own fields read the inset token:** `.ds-field` and the former Created by and Character Version pair (`.ds-field-pair > .ds-field`, gone since the two are entry rows) in `public/css/sillybunny-character-editor.css` had 16px literals, so Created by stood 4px right of the notes field once the non-row children of a settings group took 12px (`tests/character-editor-rendering.e2e.js`).
  - **Probe with the CSSOM, not by reading rules:** `getComputedStyle` says what a row got, and a walk over `document.styleSheets` for rules that match the element and set `min-height`, `padding` or `gap` says who set it. Run on an audit surface, it found each of the above in one pass.
  - **A rewritten root block keeps every token it held:** `4e03c17e8` rewrote the `:root` density block of `sillybunny-theme.css` and dropped `--sb-icon-control-radius`. The token stayed declared only on `.sb-header-icon-button` (`sillybunny-editor-shell.css`), so the icon-only `.menu_button` controls, `.editor_maximize`, the inline drawer icons and the editor icon buttons that read it without a fallback drew square corners, while the prompt manager's copy with a `10px` fallback stayed round. The token is declared at the root again, and `tests/density-tokens-css.test.js` ("shared tokens") fails when a token that two or more shared sheets read without a fallback has no root declaration. To find what an undeclared token changes, compare `getComputedStyle` of every element before and after `:root { --name: initial; }`.
- **The archive writes its filter to the server (F-302):** typing in the archive search (`list.archive` search-results types `audit-archive-2`) makes it write `_sbca_organization.json` (`lastView`, debounced 500 ms) through `/api/files/upload`, which `protectMaintainerServer()` does not stub. Any later archive boot on that server loaded it ("1 matching chats for…", "1 of 9 indexed chat files"). `bootSurfaceApp()` calls `keepArchiveOrganizationOffServer()` (`tests/helpers/surfaces/boot.js`): the upload is answered without writing and the file reads as 404. `tests/audit/boot-isolation.e2e.js` fails without it.
- **Playwright's `click()` scrolls the clipped page behind a dialog:** the `install-extension` open step clicked `#third_party_extension_button`, and the scroll-into-view moved the clipped `div.sb-subpage` (scrollLeft 12) and the scroller (scrollTop 24), so the Extensions page showed shifted sideways behind the dialog in 3 of 4 runs alone. The step uses `openExtensionsList()` and a DOM click (`dialog-form.js`). A comparison that covers only the dialog box lists such a shift as "outside the compared box". Other bare `openTab('right', …)` opens (`dialog-form.js`, `dialog-window.js`, `dialog-alert.js`, `list-steps.js`, `menu.js`, `menu-steps.js`, `editor.js`) were not changed.
- **A capture cell and a retake start from a loaded page:** with `CAPTURE_BOOT=reuse` the cells of one surface and viewport share a page, and a retake ran on the page of the first pass. The state that a cell left (a typed search, a filter, selection mode, an open wand menu) made the next open step wait for what never showed: a toggle-style open (`openLegacyToggle()` on `#extensionsMenuButton`) closed the open menu and `settleFade()` waited for the test timeout (`list.persona` search-results, `list.worldinfo-entries` and `status.agents.no-results` in Libadwaita Light, `menu.composer.wand` in a retake). `rebootSurfaceApp()` (`boot.js`) reloads the page, accepts a `beforeunload` prompt (an editor with a draft asks, and a dialog nobody answers cancels the reload), and applies the variant; `loadAgain()` in `tests/audit/surface-capture.e2e.js` uses it for each cell after the first and before each retake, and adds `CAPTURE_INJECT_CSS` again. A retake also runs the surface's `close` step after a dry pass. Baselines of non-first variant cells that were recorded on a reused page can hold the state of the cell before (the as-found Libadwaita Light `list.persona` was captured in selection mode) and are recorded again.
- **A picker that is rebuilt is hidden for a frame:** `refreshOpenPersonaPicker()` (`public/scripts/sillybunny-tabs.js`) removed the open persona picker and built a new one on a persona or settings event, hidden at 0,0 until the next frame. `prepareForCapture()` (`tests/helpers/capture-page.js`) checked the root right after it blurred the page, found it hidden and opened the surface again, and the open step's click on the bubble closed the picker that was still there (`#sb-persona-picker: none`, 1 to 5 % of captures under load). The new picker takes the old one's position (`place`) and stays shown; `tests/persona-picker-dismiss.e2e.js` reads it on six frames after `PERSONA_UPDATED`. `openPersonaMenu()` clicks the bubble only when no picker exists. Refreshing still drops the focus that was inside the picker.
- **A heading in a titled menu section fails `aria-required-children`:** `buildMenu()` (`public/scripts/sillybunny-action-menu.js`) wraps a titled section in `role="group"` with an `h3` before its items, and axe (4.14.0) reports "Element has children which are not allowed: h3" on the `role="menu"` (a bare reproduction of the markup; a group without the heading, or with the heading as `role="presentation"`, passes). Every menu with a titled section had the record: `menu.tag-manager.sort` (new when 6.7 moved the sort into the search bar), `menu.character-list.sort`, `menu.chats-archive.header`, `menu.chats-archive.view`, `menu.home.main` and the `home.home` main-menu state (10 records at both sizes, removed). The title is `role="presentation"` now; the group is still named through `aria-labelledby`. It stays an `h3` on purpose: the theme's boxed `h3` rule (`sillybunny-theme.css`, with an id inside a `:not()`) outranks `.sb-action-menu-section-title`, so the title draws at title size with 10px 14px padding, and a `div` drew the caption style that the class rule says (12px bold, 32px start padding) and moved the rows of five menus. Test: `tests/unit/sillybunny-action-menu.test.js`.
- **A phone's submenu can lie over the item that opened it:** the Quick Reply context menu opens a submenu beside its parent item and, when neither side has room, flips it left and clamps it to the window (`SubMenu.place()`). In 390px the menu (207px) and a submenu (208px) cannot sit side by side, and 6a26480c2 (raised single-line Quick Reply buttons) moved the last button to the left of a wrapped tray, so its menu opened at x 88 and the submenu (x 5 to 213) covered the middle of the parent item (x 95 to 288). Playwright's `hover()` then found the item covered and retried to the 90 s stall of `menu.quick-reply.context` (found with a bisect between `14a0cfc38` and `ae7d9c2bb`; the parent of 6a26480c2 is good). A touch opens the submenu through the item's `⋮` expander, which stays clear of the submenu, so the step taps it on a coarse pointer and hovers on a fine one; the app is unchanged.

### Interface page, device settings and the pin

- **Breaks:** a setting that has one stored value but one switch per device (Compact Mode had `#sb-desktop-compact-mode-input` and `#sb-mobile-compact-mode-input`, both writing `sb-compact-mode`), a pin that stores a value and has no effect at some widths, and a list that upstream turns into `display: contents`.
- **Files:** `public/scripts/sillybunny-tabs.js` (`injectThemePicker()`, `createCompactModeRow()`, `createCharactersPinRow()`), `public/scripts/sillybunny-settings-content.js` (`SB_SETTINGS_SECTION_ORDER.interface`), `public/scripts/sillybunny-settings-presentation.js` (`adoptButtonRows()`, `adoptGroupHeadings()`, `unwrapLoneSelects()`), `public/scripts/sillybunny-character-list.js`, `public/scripts/RossAscends-mods.js` (`OpenNavPanels()`).
- **One value, one switch:** the Interface page keeps `sb-compact-mode` behind `#sb-compact-mode-input` in its first group. The Bottom Bar group of that page holds Bottom Bar Size and one switch each for the bottom chat bar (`sb-bottom-chat-bar-visible`, `setBottomChatBarVisible`) and the Guided Generations bar (`extension_settings['guided-generations'].showActionButtonContainer`), whatever the device; their ids keep the `desktop` segment (`#sb-desktop-bottom-bar-visible-input`, `#sb-desktop-gg-bar-visible-input`) because the settings inventory and the search specs name them.
- **The pin applies above 768px only:** the Characters pin is upstream's `NavLockOn` (accountStorage, `'true'` or `'false'`), driven through the hidden `#rm_button_panel_pin` so that upstream's click handler sets `pinnedOpen`. At 768px and narrower neither the character list nor the auto-open on load reads it (`LAYOUT_MOBILE_QUERY` in `sillybunny-editor/breakpoints.js`), and the stored value is left alone. `window.matchMedia` is undefined in jsdom, so a unit that reaches that check mocks it (`tests/unit/sillybunny-character-list.test.js`).
- **`display: contents` from upstream:** `public/style.css` sets `#user-settings-block [name="MiscellaneousToggles"]` to `display: contents`, and the same-specificity rule in `sillybunny-theme.css` that restores a flex column is not dead: without it the Miscellaneous list has no box and `visible()` in `tests/helpers/settings-inventory-collect.js` reads every control in it as clipped (five controls went `hidden` in a recording).
- **A hidden dependent set has no enabling switch to reach:** `enableDependentSets()` in `tests/helpers/settings-inventory-steps.js` skips a `[data-sb-enabled-by]` group that has no box on the page (Paper Texture is for phones), instead of waiting four seconds for a switch that cannot be clicked.
- **The hidden transports are hidden once:** `#rm_PinAndTabs` and `#CharListButtonAndHotSwaps` keep the upstream ids that scripts look up, and one `display: none !important` rule in `sillybunny-tabs.css` hides them in every menu type and width. `tests/sillybunny-settings-consistency.e2e.js` reads their display in each menu type. The rules that once styled them (the tab strip, the token readout, the pin, the selected-character title, the hot swap strip and the Close Characters button, in `style.css`, `mobile-styles.css`, `toggle-dependent.css`, `sillybunny-tabs.css` and `sillybunny-conversation.css`) are gone: an element in a `display: none` parent needs none. `#character_import_button` and `#external_import_button` are in the list header, not in the transports.
- **Paper Texture is phone-only on purpose:** `index.html` links `css/sillybunny-paper-theme.css` with `media="(max-width: 768px)"`, so the overlay never paints on the desktop layout and its group keeps `sb-mobile-setting`. The 4.17 audit read the overlay's rule without the link's `media` attribute and reported a setting with no control on the desktop (F-6); the user rejected the finding. Read the stylesheet link in `index.html` before you call a device split a defect.
- **The Top Bar Label is one list at every width:** `sb-topbar-label-desktop-parts` (a JSON array, default `["char"]`) holds the parts for the desktop and the phone layout, and the Top Bar Label group (`#sb-topbar-label-drawer`, three switch rows) and the Custom Text group show at every width. A browser that has no list but has `sb-topbar-label-mobile-part` gets a list of that one part (`''` and an unknown value give `[]`, the default label) from `readTopbarLabelParts()`; `seedTopbarLabelParts()` writes it once at start, so the phone key is never read again and never written. A mix is cut with an ellipsis at 390px (the title has about 78px), as a long single name is. Tests: `tests/topbar-label-persistence.test.js`, `tests/sillybunny-selection-fixes.e2e.js`.
- **The phone has no icons-only value:** `isTopbarIconsOnlyActive()` is `!isMobileViewport() && sbState.topbarIconsOnly.desktop`; `sb-mobile-topbar-icons-only` stays in browser storage and nothing reads it. `setTopbarIconsOnly('mobile', …)` is a no-op, because `window.SillyBunnyShell.setTopbarIconsOnly` is public. A phone bar with that key `true`, `false` or absent has the same boxes (`tests/topbar-layout.e2e.js`, "draws the same bar whichever icons-only value"); read the bar after a reload, because the first load of a page measures the title before the fonts settle (57px against 78px).
- **The top bar scale keys are retired, not deleted:** `sb-topbar-scale-desktop` and `sb-topbar-scale-mobile` stay in browser storage, unread and unwritten (their sliders went in v1.3.2, `10f2b91e4`, and `bbe73444e`, and no control replaced them, so a stored value was stuck). Nothing in `sillybunny-tabs.js` sets `--sb-topbar-scale-desktop` or `--sb-topbar-scale-mobile`, so the stylesheet's `1` is always in force. `getTopbarScale()` on the shell object returns `100` for extensions and `setTopbarScale()` is a no-op. Do not add the keys to `sillybunny-retired-keys.js` (it deletes keys). Guarded by `tests/topbar-scale-retired.test.js`.
- **Slider Mode is one choice over two upstream inputs:** `createSliderModeRow()` (`sillybunny-slider-mode.js`) is a toggle row of None, Zen Sliders and Mad Lab Mode. `#enableZenSliders` and `#enableLabMode` stay in the document, hidden (`presentSliderModeRow()` in `sillybunny-tabs.js`), because their `input` handlers in `power-user.js` write `power_user.enableZenSliders` and `power_user.enableLabMode`. `chooseSliderMode()` turns the other mode off first and waits 150ms before it turns the new one on, because `switchZenSliders()` and `switchLabMode()` change the page after a 100ms delay and `switchLabMode()` off resets every slider's limits; written the other way round, upstream's rejection toast fires. Tests: `tests/unit/sillybunny-slider-mode.test.js`.
- **Quick Access is a desktop group:** every Quick Access button on the bar carries `sb-desktop-setting` (hidden below 769px with `!important`), and `docs/feature-design-rules.md` calls it desktop-only, so `createShortcutSettingsGroup()` gives the group (`#sb-quick-access-shortcuts-drawer`) that class; the phone page offers no Quick Access rows.
- **Mobile Quick Actions are gone:** the setting and the `#sb-mobile-nav` dialog it filled were removed, because no control opened the dialog. The saved lists (`sb-mobile-quick-actions-v2`, `sb-mobile-quick-actions`, `sb-desktop-quick-actions-v2`) stay in browser storage and nothing reads, writes or deletes them (`sillybunny-retired-keys.js` must not list them). The top bar's Quick Access overflow menu (`#sb-topbar-overflow-toggle`) is a different feature and stays. `tests/sillybunny-settings-consistency.e2e.js` seeds the three keys and asserts that the page has no group and that the values survive.
- **Tests:** `tests/unit/sillybunny-interface-rows.test.js`, `tests/unit/sillybunny-settings-content.test.js`, `tests/unit/sillybunny-character-list.test.js`; `tests/sillybunny-settings-consistency.e2e.js`, `tests/character-list.e2e.js`, `tests/splash.e2e.js`.

### Expander rows

- **Breaks:** an expander built on `<details>` cannot hold a focusable control in its `<summary>` (the Persona scenario note put its Enabled switch there and stopped the click by hand), and each adopter animated and opened it its own way.
- **Files:** `createExpanderRow` and `adoptExpanderRow` in `public/scripts/sillybunny-editor/rows.js`; the `.ds-row-expander*` rules in `public/css/sillybunny-tabs.css`; adopters in `sillybunny-tabs.js` (sampler cards), `sillybunny-persona-detail.js`, `sillybunny-world-info-entry.js`, `sillybunny-character-editor.js` and the chats archive.
- **Guards:**
  - The header is a row holding a native toggle `<button>` (`aria-expanded`, `aria-controls`), a suffix and an arrow; the toggle's `::after` covers the row. Enter and Space are the button's own, so no key handler exists.
  - Closed content is `hidden`, so it is out of the tab order. Anything that walks ancestors for `hidden` must skip `.ds-row-expander-content[hidden]`: settings search made a closed sampler's controls unavailable until `computeSearchEntryAvailability` did (`tests/sillybunny-backend-settings.e2e.js`, "Native sampler expanders reveal search results").
  - The reveal is `animateHeightFrom` and `play` from `sillybunny-motion.js`, which do nothing under reduced motion. `rows.js` imports that module, so a Jest mock of it needs `animateHeightFrom`, `play`, `stopMotion` and `MOTION_SLOW`; six suites stopped linking without them.
  - Several rows may be open at once; no adopter keeps one open.
- **Tests:** `tests/unit/sillybunny-editor-rows.test.js`; `tests/foundation/shared-ui.e2e.js` (expander rows); `tests/persona-management-ui.e2e.js`.

### Floating entry rows (the row is the field)

- **Shape:** the input draws no fill, border, radius or ring; the ring (`--sb-focus-ring-inset`) is on the row through `:has(.ds-row-entry__field > input:focus)`, and a pencil (`createEntryEditIcon`) hides while the text has focus. Without a box, the row reads as a field only by libadwaita's two cues: the title in the dim colour (`--sb-muted-fg`, `.dimmed`), set apart from full-colour row titles, and the pencil as a 16px icon in the text colour; with the brighter placeholder colour and a Caption-sized dim pencil the user found the rows did not look editable at a glance (`libadwaita-surface-layers-css.test.js` holds both values). On a secret row the pencil also sat in one run of four small icons (status, pencil, Reveal, key manager) and still did not stand out; the key status now sits in the field before the pencil and a vertical separator sets the buttons apart (`ce33f705d`'s follow-up, chosen by the user from four drawn variants, `review/p4-entry-affordance/key-prototypes/`). The editable area is `--sb-entry-field-height`, the row's minimum height less its own padding, so the row keeps the canonical 6px padding the settings parity measures (the parity check of `tests/audit/surface-audit.e2e.js` reported padding 0 when the row dropped it).
- **Breaks:**
  - The shell's `:is(.sb-shell-root, …) :is(textarea, input…):focus:not([disabled]):not([readonly])` rule (`sillybunny-theme.css`, specificity 0,5,1) outranks a plain `…__field > input:focus` rule and drew a second ring on the input; the entry input rules name that same `:focus:not(…)` form. A fixture without a shell root does not show this: `tests/sillybunny-settings-consistency.e2e.js` ("an entry row on a settings page draws one ring round the row") runs in the real app.
  - The mounted-shell rule that sets `box-shadow: none` on rows must skip `.ds-row-entry--floating`, or the row ring never shows.
  - `#openai_api .flex-container { flex-wrap: wrap }` outranks a class selector on the Connections model field, so its nowrap rule uses `#sb-settings-page`. Upstream code adds the list and favourite buttons before the pencil on some sources and after it on others, so the pencil and the buttons take `order`.
  - Authorize carries its own `data-source` gate: put inside the mirror of the key row it met the same gate twice and `tests/sillybunny-connections-layout.e2e.js` ("gate signatures") failed, so it is a unit of its own between the description and the key row (`sillybunny-connections-groups.js`).
  - `:is(:autofill, :-webkit-autofill)` inside `:has()` is safe because `:is()` drops the spelling an engine lacks; separate rules per spelling are not needed.
  - Tests that read the focus ring from the focused element (`window.sbA11y.focusState`) read the row for an entry input, as for a spin row.
- **Open:** the phone stylesheet `sillybunny-paper-theme.css` still sets `border-radius` with `!important` on every input, so an entry input has a radius at 768px and below (nothing draws on it). A text input with a `list` shows the browser's list arrow while focused. The Caps Lock indicator that DESIGN.md describes for secret fields does not exist in the code.

### Formatting and Presets pages

- **Breaks:** rows that the generic row pass never reached (the Prompting root carries `data-sb-presentation="manual"`), upstream wrappers that Backend source rules show and hide, and old nodes that a later regroup pass moved back into a box.
- **Files:** `public/scripts/sillybunny-backend-pages.js` (loaded on demand by `ensureBackendPages` in `sillybunny-tabs.js`), `adoptFieldRow` options in `sillybunny-settings-presentation.js`, `tests/helpers/prompting-layout.js`.
- **Guards:**
  - `adoptFieldRow(row, { manual: true })` adopts rows inside a `data-sb-presentation="manual"` root; `keepWrapper` leaves a node that carries an id, a `data-` attribute or an inline style around the new row, because the Backend source switch (`[data-source]`, jQuery `.toggle()`) and `data-cc-null` act on that node. A replaced wrapper copies them with `carryVisibility`.
  - `retire(node)` moves an emptied upstream node out of its box and sets `dataset.sbEmptiedByOrder`; `move`, `movePromptingNode` and `startReplyWith` in `sillybunny-tabs.js` skip such nodes, or the next backend change put them back. A hidden sibling that stays in a box leaves its neighbour with a separator that `:first-child` and `:last-child` cannot remove (`#sb-openai-request` keeps an audit allowlist entry for its source-hidden rows).
  - The template buttons (`[data-preset-manager-*="<api>"]` and the file input) stay in the document inside `[data-sb-template-actions="<api>"]`, because `preset-manager.js` delegates its click handlers from `document`; the Template Actions menu clicks them. Rows that hold a template select carry `data-sb-preset-row`, which the autosave flush reads.
  - The Enable Instruct Mode and Enable System Prompt switches do not disable their groups, because a template could not be picked while the switch is off; upstream toggles `.disabled` on `#instructSettingsBlock` and `#SystemPromptBlock`, and one rule dims the rows.
  - A link row that opens another group passes the group's title to `revealSearchMatch`, which centres its target: a tall group would centre below its own heading (`tests/sillybunny-backend-settings.e2e.js`, "open each other").
  - **Character Names Behavior and Continue Postfix are select rows over their radios** (`createRadioSelectRow()`): a mirror `<select>` (`#sb-names-behavior-select`, `#sb-continue-postfix-select`) is the row's control and the radios stay in the document, hidden, in their own labels, because `openai.js` checks them with `prop('checked')` and no event on a preset load and binds `input` on each, and `profile-change-notice.js` reads a label through `radio.closest('label')`. A pick checks the radio and fires `input` and `change` on it; the row follows the radios through their `change`, through the readout (`#character_names_display`, `#continue_postfix_display`) that upstream rewrites after every load, and through a capture click on the trigger. The note of an option shows under the row title (`mapItem`). Tests: `tests/unit/sillybunny-radio-select-row.test.js`, `tests/sillybunny-selection-fixes.e2e.js`.
  - Their two groups have no visible heading (`createGroup({ headless: true })`), because each row carries the title; the box is a `role="group"` named by `aria-label`. A test that lists groups by their headings misses them: five tests of `tests/sillybunny-backend-settings.e2e.js` failed at `974342ccc` until `groupNames()` read that label, as the group helper of `tests/sillybunny-settings-consistency.e2e.js` does.
  - `auditPromptingLayout` measures the control of a `.ds-row-check` row (radio or check) at the 12px inset, and the text of every other row.
- **Tests:** `tests/sillybunny-backend-settings.e2e.js`, `tests/sillybunny-settings-consistency.e2e.js`, `tests/sillybunny-preset-autosave.e2e.js`; the inventory and parity specs under `tests/audit/`.

### Message rows (action row, swipe controls, delete mode)

- **Breaks:**
  - The set of actions in a row followed `max-width: 768px`, so a fine pointer at 390px got the phone set and a coarse pointer at 844px the desktop set. It follows `(pointer: coarse)` now: `public/css/sillybunny-message-actions.css`, `COARSE_POINTER_QUERY` in `public/scripts/sillybunny-message-actions.js`, and the `--sb-message-icon-size` and `--sb-message-glyph-size` overrides in `sillybunny-theme.css`. A rule of the row that is keyed on a width moves the action set again.
  - Upstream dims every row control with `opacity: 0.3` (`public/style.css`: `.mes_button`, `.swipe_left`, `.swipe_right`, `.swipes-counter`), about 2.6:1 on the dark tone (Delete 1.8:1). The rest state is `color: var(--sb-muted-fg)` at opacity 1 in both tones, except on `.mes.last_swipe`, which keeps upstream's highlight. An opacity rule or an `!important` opacity on a counter brings the contrast back down.
  - `updateEditArrowClasses()` (`public/script.js`) compared the edited message with the first and last rendered ones, so in a chat longer than `chat_truncation` the first rendered message had Move Up disabled. It compares with `0` and `chat.length - 1`, and the Move Up handler calls `showMoreMessages()` first when the message above is not rendered, because `messageEditMove()` needs both messages in the DOM.
  - The theme's `display: inline-flex` on `.mes_button` outranks the base rules that hide the inactive media toggle, so both Gallery and List showed (`.mes[data-media-display]`) and the expanded row wrapped. `#chat .mes:not([data-media-display='gallery']) .mes_media_gallery` and its List twin hide it.
  - `.mes_text { overflow-wrap: anywhere }` shrinks a table's min-content to one letter, so Echo and Ripple broke the words in table headers at 390px; `th` and `td` take `break-word`.
  - The Moonlit styles grouped the previous swipe arrow with the counter at 768px and narrower, and a later `right: auto !important` made desktop accidentally split it; the previous arrow is at the start edge at every width, and the size is `--sb-message-hit-size`.
  - Whisper and Hush drew a 3px `::after` stripe per role. The row tint alone tells the role now.
- **Guards:**
  - The first test of the popover `tests/action-menu-viewport.e2e.js` (`the message menu stays inside the window`) failed 10 of 10 alone at the base and passed in a grouped run: a chat load focuses `#send_textarea` after `debounce_timeout.short` (`public/script.js`, `CHAT_LOADED`), and the popover closes when focus leaves it. `seedChat` waits for the composer to take focus.
  - The Delete bar's markup is Cancel, `#dialogue_del_mes_count` (`role="status"`), Delete, and `initializeDeleteCount()` writes `${count} selected` from the `selected` class that upstream sets. A click on a message selects it and every message after it, which is upstream's behaviour.
  - On Whisper the controls sit on a banner picture, so they take the OSD fill and ink (`--sb-osd-bg`, `--sb-osd-ink` in `sillybunny-theme.css`) with the edit actions' role colours given up, because the light tone's role colours are dark and measured 1.0 to 1.6:1 on the fill. A Document message that is not the last has its row as an overlaid toolbar on a fine pointer, in the flow on a coarse one; its rule must set `inline-size: max-content`, since a narrow-width rule stretches `.mes_buttons` over the whole message and then it intercepted clicks on the reasoning header.
  - `sillybunny-chat-styles.css` is injected for every chat style (`ensureNativeChatStyleStylesheet()` in `power-user.js`), so it is outside the blocking budget: the Document, Whisper, delete-mode and counter hit-area rules are there.
- **Tests:** `tests/foundation/message-actions.e2e.js`; `tests/message-actions-contrast.test.js`; `tests/chat-scroll-regressions.e2e.js` (`redisplay keeps render follow-up hooks applied after batched render` expects Move Up enabled on the first rendered message of a longer chat); the audit spec for `chat.roleplay.*` and `menu.message.roleplay`.

### Conversation timeline (message toolbar, start pickers, composer focus)

- **Breaks (toolbar and text):**
  - A hover toolbar rises above the name row, so on a message with a name it covered the name and time (50 of 50 header messages in a probe at 1440, 1024, 768, 390 and 320 wide), and a second row covered the message above (21). `.sb-conversation-timeline` is a container (`sb-conv-timeline`): at 512px or more the toolbar may be 30rem wide on one row; narrower, the `[data-sb-secondary]` actions leave the toolbar for the menu (which builds from the toolbar's buttons, so it still holds them), and under 340px the reactions go too. `syncMetaClearance()` in `message-menu.js` sets `data-sb-meta-covered` on a header message when the toolbar box would reach the name or time, and the CSS hides the name row while the toolbar shows. After this the probe found 0 of 50 covered and 0 two-row toolbars.
  - Known residual: a continuation message has no name row, so its toolbar still covers the trailing text of the bubble above (17 of 20 probe cases). Reserving a lane was refused for density; a probe that counts only overlap with the bubble's text misses the name and time, so measure all timeline text.
  - A fine pointer's group start margin and the day divider's bottom margin are 12px (was 8), so the toolbar clears the text above.
- **Breaks:**
  - The toolbar and the trigger beside the bubble followed `max-width: 768px`, so a touch tablet at 1024px got a two-row toolbar of 44px buttons over the message text, and a mouse at 390px got the trigger. They follow `(pointer: coarse)` now: `COARSE_POINTER_QUERY` in `public/scripts/sillybunny-conversation/message-menu.js` and the coarse block of `public/css/sillybunny-conversation.css`. A Conversation test that needs the trigger at 390px runs with `hasTouch: true, isMobile: true`; a default context at that width has the toolbar.
  - The toolbar shares the bubble's grid cell (`grid-area: 1 / 1`), so its box, though translated away and transparent, sets the minimum height of every single-line bubble (38px). A change to the toolbar's padding or button size moves every bubble in the timeline.
  - `--sb-focus-ring-inset` is resolved at `:root`, so setting `--sb-focus-ring-color` on the toolbar changes nothing; the toolbar writes its own `box-shadow` ring (`--sb-osd-focus-ring`).
  - The solo and group start pickers (`toggleAddDmPicker`, `toggleConversationGroupPicker` in `pickers.js`) are the shared `openPicker`, which toggles shut when it is opened again from its own trigger. Both close the open start picker first, or a switch between solo and group would close instead of switch.
  - The composer was focused after 100ms, which is neither the render flush that enables it nor the lazy sheet that shows it. `afterConversationRender()` in `render-scheduler.js` runs a callback after each flush until it returns true, and `focusConversationInputAfterRender()` in `chrome.js` waits on `isConversationStylesheetLoading()`. A new timer for this brings the race back.
- **Guards:** a footer item of the shared picker takes `disabled` as a boolean or a function; the function is read again after each selection (`syncFooter()` in `picker.js`), which is how Create Group stays off until two members are chosen.
  - A `multiple` picker takes an optional `selectionCount: () => string`, shown in a `role=status` line (`.sb-picker__count`) and rewritten after each pick only when its text changes (`syncSelectionCount()` in `picker.js`), so a screen reader hears a pick and not a re-render. The group picker uses it for `N selected`, locked members included.
- **Tests:** `tests/sillybunny-conversation-mobile-controls.test.js`; `tests/sillybunny-conversation-render-scheduler.test.js`; `tests/conversation-start-picker.e2e.js`; `tests/conversation-message-actions.e2e.js`; `tests/conversation-action-sheet-focus.e2e.js`; `tests/foundation/conversation-mode.e2e.js`; the audit spec for `chat.conversation.timeline`, `menu.message.conversation` and `status.conversation.*`.

### Conversation settings pane (layout, shared rows)

- **Breaks (follow-ups):**
  - The pages layout followed `@media (max-width: 768px)`, but `#sheld` is about 55% of the viewport, so at 1024x600 the composer entry was 53px wide and from 769px to 1169px it was 34 to 282px. `layoutForSheld()` returns `pages` when the viewport is 768px or less or `#sheld` is under `TWO_PANE_MIN_WIDTH` (670px: 288px of composer buttons, a 160px entry, the 220px rail and 2px of frame); the pages block of the CSS selects `#sheld[data-sb-conversation-layout='pages']`, so JS and CSS cannot disagree. The header gear is hidden in `pages`, so the conversation menu's Conversation settings item follows `isConversationPagesLayout()` and not a media query, or settings was unreachable from 769px to 1169px.
  - An overlay pane had no shade, and a press on the timeline passed through it. `#sb_conversation_settings_backdrop` is now the shield of an overlay pane: it covers all of `#sheld` (z-index raised + 3, the pane raised + 4), is half the dialog backdrop, shows only in the `overlay` layout (`syncConversationPage()`), and a press on it only calls `dismissConversationSettings()`. A `split` pane is a column of the layout and stays open on a press elsewhere. Escape closes an overlay pane from anywhere in `#sheld` (a `document` handler that runs after the search and rail handlers), and focus returns to the opener captured at open, else the gear, else the conversation menu button. There is no focus trap and the content is not `inert`.
  - The pane's shadow is `--sb-conv-pane-shadow`, because a literal `0 0 56px` raised the design-rule `shadow-literal` count.
  - The header title was named by `aria-label` `name, branches`, which failed Label in Name; the label is gone, the visible name is the accessible name and `title="Branches"` is its description. Pickers are bottom sheets under `MENU_SHEET_QUERY`, as every menu.
  - A test that counts clicks that reach the content under the shade must reset the count after opening the pane, because the gear is in the header it listens on.
- **Breaks:**
  - `settings-panel.js` chose the docked layout by `window.innerWidth >= 1200`, but at 1440x900 `#sheld` is about 790px wide, so the 360px pane and the 220px rail left the timeline about 210px and the composer input 34px. `layoutForSheld()` docks the pane (`split`) only when `#sheld` itself is at least 1120px wide and otherwise lays it over the timeline (`overlay`), limited to `100% - var(--sb-conv-sidebar-width)` so it never covers the rail.
  - `.sb-conversation-switch` (specificity 0,1,0) lost to the global checkbox rules, so the pane's booleans drew as plain checkboxes. The pane is built from the shared rows (`buildSettingsDrawerContent()` in `timeline-render.js` with `rows.js` and `select-row.js`), and its booleans are `createSwitchRow` rows with `role=switch`.
  - A group heading at level 3 is boxed by the theme: `h3:not(.popup h3):not(.sb-dialog h3):not(#chat h3)` in `sillybunny-theme.css` gives every `h3` outside a dialog and the chat a border, padding and fill, and its `#chat` argument outranks a class-scoped fix. The pane's groups use `createPrefGroup({ headingLevel: 4 })`.
  - The mute listener was bound once to the pre-built drawer and lost when the drawer was rebuilt on open, so muting stopped disabling Priority. The Mute row's `onChange` (`updateConversationNotificationSettingsVisibility`) does it now.
- **Tests:** `tests/foundation/conversation-mode.e2e.js` (overlay at 1440 with the name left of the pane, `split` at 2400, `role=switch`); `tests/sillybunny-conversation-mobile-controls.test.js` (overlay width, coarse header avatar); `tests/conversation-lorebook-hint.e2e.js`; `tests/libadwaita-states-css.test.js` (no local switch rule).

### Target size, accessible names and roles (cross-cutting)

- **Breaks:**
  - A range input was 4px tall (`input[type='range']` in `sillybunny-theme.css`), so no slider reached the target floor and a press beside the trough missed it. The input is `var(--sb-density-floor)` tall and draws the 4px trough as a centred gradient (`--sb-range-trough`), so the track pseudo-elements need no rule and the thumb sits in the middle in Blink, Gecko and WebKit. Upstream's `.neo-range-slider { margin: 7px 0 0 !important }` offset a thin element; on the taller one it grew every slider row by 7px, so the `!important` rule sets `margin: 0`. The double-ended slider (`.doubleRangeInputContainer`) stays 4px, because its fill is placed against that height.
  - A control that shows its value (Role, Category, Position) was named by its label alone, so axe `label-content-name-mismatch` (Label in Name, WCAG 2.5.3) fired on it. `nameByValue(control, label, value)` in `sillybunny-editor/chrome.js` names it `<label>: <value>`; call it again whenever the value changes. `setSummaryText()` takes the text without the chevron and appends an `aria-hidden` `.sb-editor-summary__chevron`, so `textContent` still ends in `›` and the accessible name does not: a test that builds the expected name from `textContent` strips the chevron.
  - A property that is a switch is one `<label>` (`label.sb-prompt-property`, floor tall), so a press on its text toggles it.
  - The shared `a.note-link-span` floor missed the help links, because the markup is `a.notes-link > span.note-link-span` and the span is the 28px box; the floor selects `.notes-link:has(> .note-link-span)` and the other help-link forms.
  - The top bar's `.drawer-icon` and the extension controls that `#sb-topbar-extension-slot` adopts are bare divs. `a11y.js` gives the ones in `topBarControlSelectors` a button role and their tooltip as the label, as it does for the composer.
  - `$('body').toggleClass('tts', extension_settings.tts.enabled)` in `extensions/tts/index.js` flips the class when the setting is `undefined` (never switched), so Narrate All Chat showed with TTS off. It passes `Boolean(...)`.
  - The Home, Customize and Characters header toggles have tooltips such as `Return to the landing page (Ctrl+O)`, so the accessible name did not contain the visible text and axe fired `label-content-name-mismatch`. `createProxyButton()` in `sillybunny-tabs.js` takes a `name` (Home, Customize, Characters) for `aria-label` and keeps the title as the description. `tests/topbar-upgrade.e2e.js` checks `toHaveAccessibleName` and that the title still ends in `(Ctrl+X)`.
  - The wand menu's `.extension_container` wrappers were a tab stop with no role (`aria-required-children` on `#extensionsMenu`). `keyboard.js` skips them with `:not(.extension_container)`, and `prompt-inspector/index.js` no longer forces `interactable` and `tabIndex` on its wrapper; the inner `.list-group-item` rows keep their listitem role. `tests/composer-menu-sheets.e2e.js` asserts that no wrapper has a tabindex, role or `interactable` class.
  - The extension manager's switches are 24px tall inside rows that must not grow, so their hit area is an invisible `::before` on `.extensions_info .extension_toggle input[type="checkbox"]` (`extensions-panel.css`). `style.css` already draws the tick in that checkbox's `::before`; the rule resets `background`, `clip-path` and `transform` so the tick does not move or grow. Check with `document.elementFromPoint`, not the box size.
  - `.ds-select-row__trigger` is floor tall and wide through `--sb-density-floor` in `sillybunny-tabs.css`, with no coarse-only override; `.sbca-filters .ds-select-row__trigger` in the chats archive sets `min-inline-size: 0` and so beats the width floor.
  - The Quick Reply, Guided Generations, Input History and regex stylesheets are loaded by the extension loader without a `?v=` key, so they have no cache key to bump. Their buttons, grips and checkbox labels take `--sb-density-floor` (the QR ones through `--qr--target`).
  - A button that shows an icon and a label breaks DESIGN.md Buttons, Content (an icon or a label, not both). 60 were found by a read of the code; a probe that opened every registered surface found which of them render. Not every `menu_button_icon` with a `<span>` is reachable: the Prompt Manager footer and its entry popup (`display: none !important` under `.sb-prompt-owned`, `public/css/sillybunny-prompt-editor.css`), the Chat History label tools (their group is `hidden`, and the menu items click them), the Connection Manager's nine profile buttons (inside `.sb-connections-legacy`, which the Connections page hides), and the `delete_alternate_greeting` template (the editor builds its own icon-only rows in `_createGreetingRow`) are transports or templates, so their markup stays. Check that a node is shown before you change its form.
  - A `<button class="menu_button">` is `width: min-content` in `public/style.css`, so removing the icon from a button whose own sheet never set a width made the label wrap one word to a line (`.sb-schedule-modal-add` drew a 70px disc). Give a label-only button `width: fit-content` and `white-space: nowrap` in its surface's sheet.
  - A one-class rule (`.sb-selection-action__icon { display: none }`) did not hide the icon, which still showed at 1440px; the cause was not traced, and `.sb-selection-action > .sb-selection-action__icon` hides it. The selection-mode action bar renders its icon only when a consumer sets `iconOnlyAtNarrow`; the sheet shows it under 450px and hides the label there.
  - The speaker bar's buttons were icon-only below a 900px container, which in practice was every desktop width, because the chat column is narrower than that. Label-only buttons need about 350px, so a 450px bar wraps them under the chips; a phone held sideways (`max-height: 500px`) keeps one row and scrolls the chips instead (`tests/composer-bar-controls.e2e.js`, "the chat keeps room to read with the speaker bar shown").
- **Guards:**
  - `tests/unit/sillybunny-button-forms.test.js` fails when a listed button carries an icon and a label, or when an icon-only button lacks a tooltip equal to its accessible name. The table is `BUTTON_FORMS` in `tests/helpers/button-forms.js`; add a row for each button that moves to the rule.
  - The design-rule audit counts `margin: -1px 0` as an off-scale literal and does not look inside `calc()`.
  - `public/style.css` pins the height, margin and background of `.neo-range-slider` with `!important`, so the theme's `input[type='range'].neo-range-slider` rule repeats them with `!important`; the `.doubleRangeInputContainer` rule and the plain `input[type='range']` rule need none.
- **Tests:** `tests/touch-target-floors-css.test.js`; `tests/unit/top-bar-control-roles.test.js`; `tests/tts-body-class.test.js`; `tests/unit/sillybunny-editor-chrome-navigation.test.js` (`nameByValue`, the chevron); `tests/topbar-upgrade.e2e.js`; `tests/composer-menu-sheets.e2e.js`.

### Flat column surface and the composer row (bottom bar, Guided Generations fold)

- **Breaks:**
  - Flat painted a fill per message (`.mes_block`), which left the gaps between messages and the avatar column showing the raw background, two tones in edit mode (`--sb-editing-bg` under the avatar, the block tint beside it), and a selected message (`#chat .mes.selected { background-color: rgb(102, 0, 0) }` in `public/style.css`) red only around the block. Flat has no fill per message now: `body.flatchat #chat` and `.sb-message-screenshot-surface` take `linear-gradient(var(--sb-flat-column-tint), var(--sb-flat-column-tint))` in `sillybunny-chat-styles.css`, and the two Document selectors there carry `:not(.flatchat)`. A rule that reads `.mes_block`'s background in Flat reads nothing.
  - On a window wider than 768px the column tint's alpha is multiplied by `--sb-page-card-opacity` (`rgb(from ... / calc(alpha * ...))` under `@supports`), so it follows Background Visibility. Document's fill does not: its blocks stay at the tint's own alpha at every slider value, so `tests/chat-column-transparency.e2e.js` reads the fade on Flat only.
  - Measured with a scratch spec (screenshot with the text made transparent, the pixel behind each glyph): over a bright picture Azure's column is 1.1 to 1.5:1 before and after (its chat tint is 0.2 alpha), Solarized Light 5.1 to 5.4:1 with a picture and 6.4:1 without, the Libadwaita themes 12.5 to 17:1 and opaque at the default Background Visibility.
  - The empty `#gg-qr-container` (`flex: 1 1 min(100%, 260px)`) held back 260px of the input-history and Guided Generations row, so the buttons wrapped onto a third row at a column of 423 to 550px. It is `display: none` while it holds no `.qr--button`, and at a composer container under 680px it takes its own row (`order: 2; flex-basis: 100%`).
  - `#form_sheld` is a size container (`container: sb-composer / inline-size`) only when `data-sb-mobile-ui-mode` is not `mobile`: layout containment would make it the containing block of the fixed-position menu sheets. The bar and the Guided Generations row read the chat column's width, not the window's, because Page Width and docked side panels move it (a column of 423px at a 769px window, 390px at 950px with both panels).
  - The theme sets a `display` on `#send_form :is(.gg-action-button, .stih--button).menu_button`, so a button folded into the overflow menu (`hidden`) kept showing. `#send_form #gg-action-button-container .gg-action-button[hidden]` outranks it without `!important` (the design-rule ratchet counts `!important`). `getComputedStyle(button).width` is `auto` for a `display: none` button: `fitActionButtons()` reads a visible button's `offsetWidth`.
  - A bar that wraps moves a control to another row, so the hand loses its place (DESIGN.md, Header Bars and Toolbars). The bottom chat bar and the arrows + Guided Generations row never wrap. The bar's order is: the picker (`#sb-bottom-chat-select`, `min-width: 160px`) shrinks first, then the search field (`min-width: 140px`) folds into its icon (`.sb-bottom-chat-search-folded`), then the trailing buttons get `hidden` and move into the `.sb-bottom-chat-overflow` menu named "Chat actions". `fitBottomChatBar()` in `sillybunny-tabs.js` starts every pass from the unfolded form and reads `bar.scrollWidth > bar.clientWidth`, so a wider bar gets its controls back and a verdict never depends on the last one. An open folded search takes the picker's place (`.sb-bottom-chat-search-folded.sb-bottom-chat-search-open #sb-bottom-chat-select { display: none }`), because a second row for the field was a wrap. The flex line-breaking rules need no `flex-wrap`: the bar is `nowrap` with `overflow: hidden`.
  - The `max-width: 1000px` grid layout of the bar (persona and picker on one row, the buttons behind a chevron on a second) was a wrap. It and the persona-size block now read the compact bar condition (`max-width: 768px` or a coarse pointer under 450px tall, as `BOTTOM_BAR_COMPACT_QUERY`), because the chip row and the chat sheet use its unscoped rules (`.sb-bottom-chat-search-toggle`, the hidden search field) on a landscape phone. Narrowing it to 768px alone showed the search field as a second row at 844x390 coarse.
  - The phone tray is one row at 390px and 360px: `fitActionButtons()` in the Guided Generations extension no longer skips `data-sb-mobile-ui-mode='mobile'`, 3 arrows and history + 3 Guided Generations buttons + the menu button at 390px, 3 + 3 + 1 at 360px (44px targets, 3px gaps). The composer is 214px tall with the tray open at 390x844 coarse (270px when the tray wrapped to two rows), 160px with it closed.
  - The Quick Reply host (`.gg-qr-container`) keeps its own row below the narrow column (`order: 2; flex-basis: 100%`): it is a separate bar, not part of the arrows + Guided Generations row. The regular buttons container is `flex-wrap: nowrap`.
- **Guards:**
  - Flat's column tint is under `body.flatchat` only. Probing `#chat`'s `background-image`, `.mes`, `.mes_block` and `::before`/`::after` fills for all eight chat styles in four themes with no, a bright and a dark picture, after a visit to Flat each time, differed from the base in Flat alone: no other style carries the tint, none doubles it, and a switch away from Flat leaves nothing behind.
  - `#gg_overflow_button` ("Guided Generations", the extension's own name) folds trailing buttons with a `ResizeObserver` and `MutationObserver`s through a frame-coalesced `scheduleFit()`, and writes `hidden` only when it changes, so the observers do not loop. In mobile mode it measures the tray when it is open (the container is `display: none` while closed, so nothing folds until it opens). The menu opens through `import('../../sillybunny-action-menu.js')` with `keyboard` set from `event.detail === 0`; a mouse-opened popover does not take focus, so Escape needs focus moved in first.
  - The phone tray's gaps are 3px (`sillybunny-mobile-navigation.css`); the targets stay 44px on a coarse pointer.
  - The design-rule scale is 3, 6, 9, 12, 18, 24, 36px, so a 2px gap raises the ratchet. The bottom bar fold has no viewport breakpoint at all; the 1000px rules it replaced are gone from `sillybunny-tabs.css`.
- **Tests:** `tests/composer-bar-fold.e2e.js` (one row at every width, the fold order, the menu, the phone tray and its sheet); `tests/chat-column-transparency.e2e.js` (Flat column tint and its fade); `tests/core-message-transparency.test.js` (`:not(.flatchat)` selectors, cache key); `tests/mobile-composer-spacing.e2e.js`; `tests/roleplay-composer-visibility.e2e.js`; the audit spec for `chat.roleplay.flat`, `chat.bottom-bar` and `chat.composer.*`.

### Message text contrast over a background picture (bundled themes, chat styles)

- **Breaks:**
  - Measured with a scratch spec over all 56 bundled themes x 8 chat styles x (no picture, a bright and a dark picture) at the default Background Visibility, 1440x900 and 390x844 (screenshot with the text made transparent, the pixel behind each glyph, the 5th percentile of the ratio, main text plus a `q` and an `em` that the spec adds to every message): 735 of 1,344 cells were below 4.5:1 at 1440x900 and 700 at 390x844, in 50 themes. Messages with only main text hide most of it: a measurement without the `q` and `em` probes found 230 cells.
  - A theme's message tints at 0.9 alpha let 10% of a picture through, and its quote and italics colours were tuned at the floor on the surface without a picture, so a bright picture drops them under 4.5:1. The fix is theme values: message tints at 0.96 or 1 alpha in 45 themes, and the tints moved 8% toward the surface tone (darker on a dark theme, lighter on a light one) in 18 of those, where an opaque tint is itself the weak surface, because the tints are lighter than the page tone that the 0.9 tint was blended with. Azure's chat tint went from 0 to 0.75 alpha (its message tints are 0.2: the column is the surface under its text).
  - Tide painted a bubble on `.mes_text p` only; a list, quote or heading in the message text sat on the bare picture (48 themes). The bubble now covers a direct child `ol`, `ul`, `blockquote` or heading.
  - Bubbles lifted every card toward the text colour by 6% (bot) or 11% (user), which lowers the contrast of every text colour on it: 50 themes failed on Bubbles with no picture once quotes and italics were measured. The card is now the tint alone with a 1px inset ring in the text colour (10% bot, 18% user), the way Tide marks its bubbles.
  - Whisper's avatar banner is 100px tall and, on a phone, 125px (`min-height`), which put the avatar behind the first line of a message: 17 light themes failed at 390px with no picture. The phone rule no longer raises the height.
  - Character names in Echo, Whisper, Hush, Ripple and Tide had no surface of their own: a name sat on the bare picture (or, in Whisper, on the avatar banner image), and 165 of 1,176 name cells at 1440x900 (174 at 390x844) measured under 4.5:1, in Echo and Tide with a picture (47 themes) and in Whisper on a phone. A name is 0.95rem regular text, so 4.5:1 applies, not the 3:1 of large text. A text shadow is not a surface. The five styles now give `.name_text` the speaker's message tint as its own pill (`sillybunny-chat-styles.css`), painted twice: one layer of a 0.96 alpha tint still lets 4% of a bright picture through, and that took Sonokai Andromeda's user name to 4.48:1. A user's name takes the colour the theme's Custom CSS gives `.name_text` (`--customThemeColor-primary` or `-text`), not always the main text colour, so the guard reads that colour from the theme's CSS. After the change every name cell measures 4.59:1 or more. Flat, Bubbles and Document keep their names on the card.
  - A theme revision that moves a message tint must carry the tint keys in `REVISED_THEMES[name].text`, or a user who selected the theme before keeps the old tints in `settings.json` until they re-select it. `planThemeTextRepair` still compares all ten saved colours to the old palette, so a user who changed any of them keeps all of theirs. A settings file that already carries `sb_theme_text_revision` 1 is not repaired again.
  - The server replaces a pre-release copy of a bundled theme only when the copy's SHA-256 is listed. `CONTRAST_REVISED_THEME_HASHES` lists the last release's file and, as an array value, each revision that only pre-release builds shipped: the files of `cbc26b06d` for the 46 themes that later moved their tints, and the files of `6dcbac2e2` for the two Libadwaita themes. Take the hashes from `git show <commit>:<path> | sha256sum` over the theme commits since the last tag, and list them again whenever a theme file changes after a pre-release build.
- **Guards:**
  - `tests/bundled-theme-message-contrast.test.js` with `tests/helpers/theme-message-contrast.js`: the surface under the text of each chat style from the theme file (message tint over the chat column over the page tone or a stand-in for a picture), the ratio of main text, italics and quotes at 4.5:1 in every cell. The stand-ins are the colours of the darkest and brightest 5% of each picture's pixels behind the column. Against the measured cells the model differs by 0.06 on average (0.18 at the 90th percentile) and can read 0.7 higher than the render where a picture's bright spots are blurred (Azure, Celestial Macaron), so it is a floor for edits, and the rendered measurement is the check of a change.
  - A reading of `getComputedStyle(...).color` after the text is hidden catches a colour in the middle of a fade when a theme sets `transition-duration` on `*` (Verdant): read the colours before hiding.
- **Tests:** `tests/bundled-theme-message-contrast.test.js` (message text and names); `tests/sillybunny-theme-revisions.test.js` and `tests/sillybunny-theme-text-repair.e2e.js` (the saved-palette repair replaces the text colours and the three tints); `tests/bundled-theme-reconcile.test.js` (pre-release copies); `tests/bundled-theme-text-contrast.test.js` (the earlier model, no picture).

## Reverts and release problems

- Performance work caused the largest rollback: `0ecfa44e3` reverted 14 optimisation commits. iOS stayed at "Initializing", and the desktop Characters panel did not open.
- A revert can cause its own regression: `f027cd460` (#595) restored mobile CSS that a June baseline revert removed.
- Release promotion failed once. #866 promoted `staging` to `release` too early for v1.8.0, `5d49b0ad1` (#891) reverted it, and #892 added a workflow guard.
- Hotfixes followed v1.2.7, v1.2.8 and v1.8.1. A cluster of fixes from 12 to 16 April led to the v1.3.6 stability release.
- **Upstream minimum widths beat smaller fixed widths:** `body:not(.big-avatars) .avatar_collage { min-width: var(--avatar-base-width) }` (`public/css/toggle-dependent.css:213`, 50px) wins over a smaller `width`, so in Home's Recent Chats a group chat's collage was 50px beside 44px avatars and its text started 6px further in. The old Home used 60px avatars, so the minimum never showed. `.welcomeRecent .recentChat .avatar` sets `min-width: 0` (`public/css/sillybunny-home.css`). Test: `tests/home-layout.e2e.js` (group row matches an individual row).
